\begin{lexlean}{Emit}
\useglossary{lexlean.std.bool@1.2.0}
\useglossary{lexlean.std.nat@1.2.0}
\title{Natural number addition}
\begin{semanticmodule}
inductive{
  name: Phase,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: idle, fields: []},
    {name: reading, fields: []},
    {name: committing, fields: []}
  ]
}

inductive{
  name: Event,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: request, fields: []},
    {name: batchEnd, fields: []},
    {name: commitOk, fields: []},
    {name: commitFail, fields: []}
  ]
}

inductive{
  name: Action,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: nothing, fields: []},
    {name: begin, fields: []},
    {name: commit, fields: []},
    {name: sendAll, fields: []},
    {name: failAll, fields: []}
  ]
}

definition{
  name: nextPhase,
  parameters: [phase : Phase, event : Event],
  result: Phase,
  body: (match phase
    | Phase.idle => (match event
      | Event.request => (ctor Phase.reading)
      | Event.batchEnd => (ctor Phase.idle)
      | Event.commitOk => (ctor Phase.idle)
      | Event.commitFail => (ctor Phase.idle))
    | Phase.reading => (match event
      | Event.request => (ctor Phase.reading)
      | Event.batchEnd => (ctor Phase.committing)
      | Event.commitOk => (ctor Phase.reading)
      | Event.commitFail => (ctor Phase.reading))
    | Phase.committing => (match event
      | Event.request => (ctor Phase.committing)
      | Event.batchEnd => (ctor Phase.committing)
      | Event.commitOk => (ctor Phase.idle)
      | Event.commitFail => (ctor Phase.idle))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: actionOf,
  parameters: [phase : Phase, event : Event],
  result: Action,
  body: (match phase
    | Phase.idle => (match event
      | Event.request => (ctor Action.begin)
      | Event.batchEnd => (ctor Action.nothing)
      | Event.commitOk => (ctor Action.nothing)
      | Event.commitFail => (ctor Action.nothing))
    | Phase.reading => (match event
      | Event.request => (ctor Action.nothing)
      | Event.batchEnd => (ctor Action.commit)
      | Event.commitOk => (ctor Action.nothing)
      | Event.commitFail => (ctor Action.nothing))
    | Phase.committing => (match event
      | Event.request => (ctor Action.nothing)
      | Event.batchEnd => (ctor Action.nothing)
      | Event.commitOk => (ctor Action.sendAll)
      | Event.commitFail => (ctor Action.failAll))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: sendAllowed,
  parameters: [phase : Phase, event : Event],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match (actionOf phase event)
    | Action.sendAll => (match phase
      | Phase.committing => (match event
        | Event.commitOk => true
        | Event.request => false
        | Event.batchEnd => false
        | Event.commitFail => false)
      | Phase.idle => false
      | Phase.reading => false)
    | Action.nothing => true
    | Action.begin => true
    | Action.commit => true
    | Action.failAll => true),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: failAllowed,
  parameters: [phase : Phase, event : Event],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match (actionOf phase event)
    | Action.failAll => (match phase
      | Phase.committing => (match event
        | Event.commitFail => true
        | Event.request => false
        | Event.batchEnd => false
        | Event.commitOk => false)
      | Phase.idle => false
      | Phase.reading => false)
    | Action.nothing => true
    | Action.begin => true
    | Action.commit => true
    | Action.sendAll => true),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: checkCommitting,
  parameters: [phase : Phase, next : Phase, action : Action],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match next
    | Phase.committing => (match phase
      | Phase.committing => true
      | Phase.idle => false
      | Phase.reading => (match action
        | Action.commit => true
        | Action.nothing => false
        | Action.begin => false
        | Action.sendAll => false
        | Action.failAll => false))
    | Phase.idle => true
    | Phase.reading => true),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: committingFollowsCommit,
  parameters: [phase : Phase, event : Event],
  result: Bool,
  body: (checkCommitting phase (nextPhase phase event) (actionOf phase event)),
  executable: true
}

theorem{
  name: send_only_after_commit,

  statement: ((sendAllowed phase event) = true),
  proof: induction{

              constructor: batchEnd
            },
            {

              binders: [],
              constructor: commitOk
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: commitFail
            }
          ],
          scrutinee: event
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: idle
      },
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: request
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: batchEnd
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: commitOk
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: commitFail
            }
          ],
          scrutinee: event
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: reading
      },
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: request
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: batchEnd
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: commitOk
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [actionOf, sendAllowed]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: commitFail
            }
          ],
          scrutinee: event
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: committing
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: phase
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

inductive{
  name: Method,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [{name: get, fields: []}, {name: post, fields: []}, {name: other, fields: []}]
}

inductive{
  name: Shape,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: feed, fields: []},
    {name: health, fields: []},
    {name: posts, fields: []},
    {name: postId, fields: []},
    {name: postLike, fields: []},
    {name: unknown, fields: []}
  ]
}

inductive{
  name: Route,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: notFound, fields: []},
    {name: feed, fields: []},
    {name: health, fields: []},
    {name: post, fields: []},
    {name: create, fields: []},
    {name: like, fields: []}
  ]
}

definition{
  name: routeOf,
  parameters: [method : Method, shape : Shape],
  result: Route,
  body: (match method
    | Method.get => (match shape
      | Shape.feed => (ctor Route.feed)
      | Shape.health => (ctor Route.health)
      | Shape.posts => (ctor Route.notFound)
      | Shape.postId => (ctor Route.post)
      | Shape.postLike => (ctor Route.notFound)
      | Shape.unknown => (ctor Route.notFound))
    | Method.post => (match shape
      | Shape.feed => (ctor Route.notFound)
      | Shape.health => (ctor Route.notFound)
      | Shape.posts => (ctor Route.create)
      | Shape.postId => (ctor Route.notFound)
      | Shape.postLike => (ctor Route.like)
      | Shape.unknown => (ctor Route.notFound))
    | Method.other => (ctor Route.notFound)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: isWrite,
  parameters: [route : Route],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match route
    | Route.notFound => false
    | Route.feed => false
    | Route.health => false
    | Route.post => false
    | Route.create => true
    | Route.like => true),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: needsAuth,
  parameters: [route : Route],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match route
    | Route.create => true
    | Route.like => true
    | Route.notFound => false
    | Route.feed => false
    | Route.health => false
    | Route.post => false),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: checkWriteMethod,
  parameters: [method : Method, route : Route],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match route
    | Route.create => (match method
      | Method.post => true
      | Method.get => false
      | Method.other => false)
    | Route.like => (match method
      | Method.post => true
      | Method.get => false
      | Method.other => false)
    | Route.notFound => true
    | Route.feed => true
    | Route.health => true
    | Route.post => true),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: writeComesFromPost,
  parameters: [method : Method, shape : Shape],
  result: Bool,
  body: (checkWriteMethod method (routeOf method shape)),
  executable: true
}

theorem{
  name: every_write_needs_a_token,
  parameters: [route : Route],
  statement: ((isWrite route) = (needsAuth route)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [isWrite, needsAuth]}, binders: [], constructor: notFound},
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [isWrite, needsAuth]}, binders: [], constructor: feed},
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [isWrite, needsAuth]}, binders: [], constructor: health},
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [isWrite, needsAuth]}, binders: [], constructor: post},
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [isWrite, needsAuth]}, binders: [], constructor: create},
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [isWrite, needsAuth]}, binders: [], constructor: like}
    ],
    scrutinee: route
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

            },
            {

              binders: [],
              constructor: health
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [checkWriteMethod, routeOf, writeComesFromPost]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: posts
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [checkWriteMethod, routeOf, writeComesFromPost]},
              binders: [],
              constructor: postId
            },
            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [checkWriteMethod, routeOf, writeComesFromPost]},

            },
            {

            {

              binders: [],

            {
              proof: simplify{definitions: [checkWriteMethod, routeOf, writeComesFromPost]},

            },
            {

              binders: [],

            }
          ],

        },
        binders: [],

      }
    ],
    scrutinee: method
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

theorem{
  name: unknown_methods_are_not_found,
  parameters: [shape : Shape],
  statement: ((routeOf (ctor Method.other) shape) = (ctor Route.notFound)),
  proof: simplify{definitions: [routeOf]},
  axioms: [propext]
}

inductive{
  name: Flag,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [{name: yes, fields: []}, {name: no, fields: []}]
}

definition{
  name: shouldHold,
  parameters: [room : Flag, gateClosed : Flag, write : Flag, connectionHeld : Flag],
  result: Flag,
  body: (match room
    | Flag.no => (ctor Flag.no)
    | Flag.yes => (match connectionHeld
      | Flag.yes => (ctor Flag.yes)
      | Flag.no => (match gateClosed | Flag.yes => write | Flag.no => (ctor Flag.no)))),
  executable: true
}

theorem{
  name: reads_go_on_while_writes_wait,
  parameters: [room : Flag, gateClosed : Flag],
  statement: ((shouldHold room gateClosed (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.no)) = (ctor Flag.no)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [shouldHold]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [shouldHold]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: gateClosed
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: yes
      },
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [shouldHold]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [shouldHold]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: gateClosed
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: no
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: room
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

  name: a_held_connection_keeps_its_order,

            {proof: simplify{definitions: [shouldHold]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: write
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: no
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: gateClosed
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

theorem{
  name: a_closed_gate_holds_writes,

definition{
  name: gateNext,
  parameters: [closed : Flag, overLimit : Flag, copied : Flag, timedOut : Flag],
  result: Flag,
  body: (match closed
    | Flag.no => overLimit
    | Flag.yes => (match copied
      | Flag.yes => (ctor Flag.no)
      | Flag.no => (match timedOut | Flag.yes => (ctor Flag.no) | Flag.no => (ctor Flag.yes)))),
  executable: true
}

theorem{
  name: the_gate_stays_closed_until_the_log_is_copied,
  parameters: [overLimit : Flag],
  statement: ((gateNext (ctor Flag.yes) overLimit (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.no)) = (ctor Flag.yes)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
      {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
    ],
    scrutinee: overLimit
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

theorem{
  name: the_gate_closes_at_the_limit,
  parameters: [copied : Flag, timedOut : Flag],
  statement: ((gateNext (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.yes) copied timedOut) = (ctor Flag.yes)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: timedOut
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: yes
      },
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: timedOut
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: no
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: copied
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

theorem{
  name: the_gate_opens_when_the_log_is_copied,
  parameters: [overLimit : Flag, timedOut : Flag],
  statement: ((gateNext (ctor Flag.yes) overLimit (ctor Flag.yes) timedOut) = (ctor Flag.no)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: timedOut
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: yes
      },
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: timedOut
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: no
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: overLimit
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

theorem{
  name: a_timeout_always_opens_the_gate,
  parameters: [overLimit : Flag, copied : Flag],
  statement: ((gateNext (ctor Flag.yes) overLimit copied (ctor Flag.yes)) = (ctor Flag.no)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: copied
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: yes
      },
      {
        proof: induction{
          branches: [
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: yes},
            {proof: simplify{definitions: [gateNext]}, binders: [], constructor: no}
          ],
          scrutinee: copied
        },
        binders: [],
        constructor: no
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: overLimit
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

inductive{
  name: Status,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: badRequest, fields: []},
    {name: unauthorized, fields: []},
    {name: notFound, fields: []},
    {name: serverError, fields: []}
  ]
}

inductive{
  name: Failure,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: notFound, fields: []},
    {name: badId, fields: []},
    {name: postMissing, fields: []},
    {name: noBearer, fields: []},
    {name: badToken, fields: []},
    {name: badPayload, fields: []},
    {name: badJson, fields: []},
    {name: noBody, fields: []},
    {name: longBody, fields: []},
    {name: internal, fields: []}
  ]
}

definition{
  name: statusOf,
  parameters: [failure : Failure],
  result: Status,
  body: (match failure
    | Failure.notFound => (ctor Status.notFound)
    | Failure.badId => (ctor Status.badRequest)
    | Failure.postMissing => (ctor Status.notFound)
    | Failure.noBearer => (ctor Status.unauthorized)
    | Failure.badToken => (ctor Status.unauthorized)
    | Failure.badPayload => (ctor Status.unauthorized)
    | Failure.badJson => (ctor Status.badRequest)
    | Failure.noBody => (ctor Status.badRequest)
    | Failure.longBody => (ctor Status.badRequest)
    | Failure.internal => (ctor Status.serverError)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: isServerStatus,
  parameters: [status : Status],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match status
    | Status.serverError => true
    | Status.badRequest => false
    | Status.unauthorized => false
    | Status.notFound => false),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: isInternal,
  parameters: [failure : Failure],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match failure
    | Failure.internal => true
    | Failure.notFound => false
    | Failure.badId => false
    | Failure.postMissing => false
    | Failure.noBearer => false
    | Failure.badToken => false
    | Failure.badPayload => false
    | Failure.badJson => false
    | Failure.noBody => false
    | Failure.longBody => false),
  executable: true
}

theorem{
  name: only_an_internal_failure_is_a_server_error,
  parameters: [failure : Failure],
  statement: ((isServerStatus (statusOf failure)) = (isInternal failure)),
  proof: induction{
    branches: [
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: notFound
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: badId
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: postMissing
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: noBearer
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: badToken
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: badPayload
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: badJson
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: noBody
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: longBody
      },
      {
        proof: simplify{definitions: [isInternal, isServerStatus, statusOf]},
        binders: [],
        constructor: internal
      }
    ],
    scrutinee: failure
  },
  axioms: [propext]
}

inductive{
  name: EscapeClass,
  type_parameters: [],
  parameters: [],
  constructors: [
    {name: plain, fields: []},
    {name: backslash, fields: []},
    {name: unicode, fields: []}
  ]
}

definition{
  name: escapeClass,
  parameters: [octet : UInt8],
  result: EscapeClass,
  body: (if (prim equal octet (34 : UInt8) : Bool) then (ctor EscapeClass.backslash) else (if (prim equal octet (92 : UInt8) : Bool) then (ctor EscapeClass.backslash) else (if (prim equal (prim bit_and octet (224 : UInt8) : UInt8) (0 : UInt8) : Bool) then (ctor EscapeClass.unicode) else (ctor EscapeClass.plain)))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true
}

theorem{
  name: a_quote_is_escaped,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((escapeClass (34 : UInt8)) = (ctor EscapeClass.backslash)),
  proof: reflexivity{},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: a_backslash_is_escaped,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((escapeClass (92 : UInt8)) = (ctor EscapeClass.backslash)),
  proof: reflexivity{},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: the_first_control_byte_is_escaped,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((escapeClass (0 : UInt8)) = (ctor EscapeClass.unicode)),
  proof: reflexivity{},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: the_last_control_byte_is_escaped,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((escapeClass (31 : UInt8)) = (ctor EscapeClass.unicode)),
  proof: reflexivity{},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: a_space_is_plain,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((escapeClass (32 : UInt8)) = (ctor EscapeClass.plain)),
  proof: reflexivity{},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

theorem{
  name: the_top_byte_is_plain,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((escapeClass (255 : UInt8)) = (ctor EscapeClass.plain)),
  proof: reflexivity{},
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: cat3,
  parameters: [a : Bytes, b : Bytes, c : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append a b : Bytes) c : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: cat4,
  parameters: [a : Bytes, b : Bytes, c : Bytes, d : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (cat3 a b c) d : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: define,
  parameters: [name : Bytes, value : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (prim utf8_encode "#define " : Bytes) name (prim utf8_encode " " : Bytes) (prim append value (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: row2,
  parameters: [a : Bytes, b : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 a (prim utf8_encode ", " : Bytes) b (prim utf8_encode ", " : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: phaseText,
  parameters: [phase : Phase],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match phase
    | Phase.idle => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | Phase.reading => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Phase.committing => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: eventText,
  parameters: [event : Event],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match event
    | Event.request => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | Event.batchEnd => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Event.commitOk => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)
    | Event.commitFail => (prim utf8_encode "3" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: actionText,
  parameters: [action : Action],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match action
    | Action.nothing => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | Action.begin => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Action.commit => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)
    | Action.sendAll => (prim utf8_encode "3" : Bytes)
    | Action.failAll => (prim utf8_encode "4" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: methodText,
  parameters: [method : Method],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match method
    | Method.get => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | Method.post => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Method.other => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: shapeText,
  parameters: [shape : Shape],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match shape
    | Shape.feed => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | Shape.health => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Shape.posts => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)
    | Shape.postId => (prim utf8_encode "3" : Bytes)
    | Shape.postLike => (prim utf8_encode "4" : Bytes)
    | Shape.unknown => (prim utf8_encode "5" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: routeText,
  parameters: [route : Route],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match route
    | Route.notFound => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | Route.feed => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Route.health => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)
    | Route.post => (prim utf8_encode "3" : Bytes)
    | Route.create => (prim utf8_encode "4" : Bytes)
    | Route.like => (prim utf8_encode "5" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: boolText,
  parameters: [value : Bool],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (if value then (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes) else (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: escapeText,
  parameters: [class : EscapeClass],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match class
    | EscapeClass.plain => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)
    | EscapeClass.backslash => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | EscapeClass.unicode => (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: batchNames,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "PHASE_IDLE" : Bytes) (phaseText (ctor Phase.idle))) (define (prim utf8_encode "PHASE_READING" : Bytes) (phaseText (ctor Phase.reading))) (define (prim utf8_encode "PHASE_COMMITTING" : Bytes) (phaseText (ctor Phase.committing)))) (cat4 (define (prim utf8_encode "EVENT_REQUEST" : Bytes) (eventText (ctor Event.request))) (define (prim utf8_encode "EVENT_BATCH_END" : Bytes) (eventText (ctor Event.batchEnd))) (define (prim utf8_encode "EVENT_COMMIT_OK" : Bytes) (eventText (ctor Event.commitOk))) (define (prim utf8_encode "EVENT_COMMIT_FAIL" : Bytes) (eventText (ctor Event.commitFail)))) (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "ACTION_NOTHING" : Bytes) (actionText (ctor Action.nothing))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ACTION_BEGIN" : Bytes) (actionText (ctor Action.begin))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ACTION_COMMIT" : Bytes) (actionText (ctor Action.commit)))) (prim append (define (prim utf8_encode "ACTION_SEND_ALL" : Bytes) (actionText (ctor Action.sendAll))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ACTION_FAIL_ALL" : Bytes) (actionText (ctor Action.failAll))) : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: phaseRow,
  parameters: [phase : Phase],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat3 (prim utf8_encode "    {" : Bytes) (prim append (row2 (phaseText (nextPhase phase (ctor Event.request))) (phaseText (nextPhase phase (ctor Event.batchEnd)))) (row2 (phaseText (nextPhase phase (ctor Event.commitOk))) (phaseText (nextPhase phase (ctor Event.commitFail)))) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "},\n" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: actionRow,
  parameters: [phase : Phase],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat3 (prim utf8_encode "    {" : Bytes) (prim append (row2 (actionText (actionOf phase (ctor Event.request))) (actionText (actionOf phase (ctor Event.batchEnd)))) (row2 (actionText (actionOf phase (ctor Event.commitOk))) (actionText (actionOf phase (ctor Event.commitFail)))) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "},\n" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: batchTables,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (prim utf8_encode "static const uint8_t NEXT_PHASE[3][4] = {\n" : Bytes) (cat4 (phaseRow (ctor Phase.idle)) (phaseRow (ctor Phase.reading)) (phaseRow (ctor Phase.committing)) (prim utf8_encode "};\n" : Bytes)) (prim utf8_encode "static const uint8_t ACTION_OF[3][4] = {\n" : Bytes) (cat4 (actionRow (ctor Phase.idle)) (actionRow (ctor Phase.reading)) (actionRow (ctor Phase.committing)) (prim utf8_encode "};\n" : Bytes))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: routeNames,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "METHOD_GET" : Bytes) (methodText (ctor Method.get))) (define (prim utf8_encode "METHOD_POST" : Bytes) (methodText (ctor Method.post))) (define (prim utf8_encode "METHOD_OTHER" : Bytes) (methodText (ctor Method.other)))) (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "SHAPE_FEED" : Bytes) (shapeText (ctor Shape.feed))) (define (prim utf8_encode "SHAPE_HEALTH" : Bytes) (shapeText (ctor Shape.health))) (define (prim utf8_encode "SHAPE_POSTS" : Bytes) (shapeText (ctor Shape.posts)))) (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "SHAPE_POST_ID" : Bytes) (shapeText (ctor Shape.postId))) (define (prim utf8_encode "SHAPE_POST_LIKE" : Bytes) (shapeText (ctor Shape.postLike))) (define (prim utf8_encode "SHAPE_UNKNOWN" : Bytes) (shapeText (ctor Shape.unknown)))) (cat3 (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "ROUTE_NOT_FOUND" : Bytes) (routeText (ctor Route.notFound))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ROUTE_FEED" : Bytes) (routeText (ctor Route.feed))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ROUTE_HEALTH" : Bytes) (routeText (ctor Route.health)))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ROUTE_POST" : Bytes) (routeText (ctor Route.post))) (prim append (define (prim utf8_encode "ROUTE_CREATE" : Bytes) (routeText (ctor Route.create))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ROUTE_LIKE" : Bytes) (routeText (ctor Route.like))) : Bytes))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: routeRow,
  parameters: [method : Method],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (prim utf8_encode "    {" : Bytes) (cat3 (row2 (routeText (routeOf method (ctor Shape.feed))) (routeText (routeOf method (ctor Shape.health)))) (row2 (routeText (routeOf method (ctor Shape.posts))) (routeText (routeOf method (ctor Shape.postId)))) (row2 (routeText (routeOf method (ctor Shape.postLike))) (routeText (routeOf method (ctor Shape.unknown))))) (prim utf8_encode "}," : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: routeTables,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (prim utf8_encode "static const uint8_t ROUTE_OF[3][6] = {\n" : Bytes) (cat4 (routeRow (ctor Method.get)) (routeRow (ctor Method.post)) (routeRow (ctor Method.other)) (prim utf8_encode "};\n" : Bytes)) (prim utf8_encode "static const uint8_t ROUTE_NEEDS_TOKEN[6] = {" : Bytes) (cat4 (row2 (boolText (needsAuth (ctor Route.notFound))) (boolText (needsAuth (ctor Route.feed)))) (row2 (boolText (needsAuth (ctor Route.health))) (boolText (needsAuth (ctor Route.post)))) (row2 (boolText (needsAuth (ctor Route.create))) (boolText (needsAuth (ctor Route.like)))) (prim utf8_encode "};\n" : Bytes))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: flagText,
  parameters: [flag : Flag],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match flag
    | Flag.yes => (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes)
    | Flag.no => (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: holdRow,
  parameters: [room : Flag, gateClosed : Flag],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat3 (prim utf8_encode "    " : Bytes) (prim append (row2 (flagText (shouldHold room gateClosed (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.no))) (flagText (shouldHold room gateClosed (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.yes)))) (row2 (flagText (shouldHold room gateClosed (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.no))) (flagText (shouldHold room gateClosed (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.yes)))) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: gateRow,
  parameters: [closed : Flag, overLimit : Flag],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat3 (prim utf8_encode "    " : Bytes) (prim append (row2 (flagText (gateNext closed overLimit (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.no))) (flagText (gateNext closed overLimit (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.yes)))) (row2 (flagText (gateNext closed overLimit (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.no))) (flagText (gateNext closed overLimit (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.yes)))) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: flowTables,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (prim utf8_encode "// Indexed by room, closed gate, write, held connection, most significant first.\nstatic const uint8_t SHOULD_HOLD[16] = {\n" : Bytes) (cat4 (cat4 (holdRow (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.no)) (holdRow (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.yes)) (holdRow (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.no)) (holdRow (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.yes))) (prim utf8_encode "};\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "// Indexed by closed, over the limit, copied, timed out, most significant first.\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "static const uint8_t GATE_NEXT[16] = {\n" : Bytes)) (cat4 (gateRow (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.no)) (gateRow (ctor Flag.no) (ctor Flag.yes)) (gateRow (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.no)) (gateRow (ctor Flag.yes) (ctor Flag.yes))) (prim utf8_encode "};\n" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: statusCode,
  parameters: [status : Status],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match status
    | Status.badRequest => (prim utf8_encode "400, \"Bad Request\"" : Bytes)
    | Status.unauthorized => (prim utf8_encode "401, \"Unauthorized\"" : Bytes)
    | Status.notFound => (prim utf8_encode "404, \"Not Found\"" : Bytes)
    | Status.serverError => (prim utf8_encode "500, \"Internal Server Error\"" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: failureMessage,
  parameters: [failure : Failure],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (match failure
    | Failure.notFound => (prim utf8_encode "not found" : Bytes)
    | Failure.badId => (prim utf8_encode "invalid post id" : Bytes)
    | Failure.postMissing => (prim utf8_encode "post not found" : Bytes)
    | Failure.noBearer => (prim utf8_encode "missing bearer token" : Bytes)
    | Failure.badToken => (prim utf8_encode "invalid or expired token" : Bytes)
    | Failure.badPayload => (prim utf8_encode "invalid token payload" : Bytes)
    | Failure.badJson => (prim utf8_encode "malformed JSON body" : Bytes)
    | Failure.noBody => (prim utf8_encode "body is required" : Bytes)
    | Failure.longBody => (prim utf8_encode "body must be at most 500 characters" : Bytes)
    | Failure.internal => (prim utf8_encode "internal server error" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: failureRow,
  parameters: [name : Bytes, number : Bytes, failure : Failure],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (define name number) (prim utf8_encode "#define FAILURE_ROW_" : Bytes) number (cat4 (prim utf8_encode " {" : Bytes) (statusCode (statusOf failure)) (prim utf8_encode ", \"" : Bytes) (cat3 (failureMessage failure) (prim utf8_encode "\"}" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes)))),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: failureTable,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (cat4 (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_NOT_FOUND" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "0" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.notFound)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_BAD_ID" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "1" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.badId)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_POST_MISSING" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "2" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.postMissing)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_NO_BEARER" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "3" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.noBearer))) (cat4 (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_BAD_TOKEN" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "4" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.badToken)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_BAD_PAYLOAD" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "5" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.badPayload)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_BAD_JSON" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "6" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.badJson)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_NO_BODY" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "7" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.noBody))) (prim append (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_LONG_BODY" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "8" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.longBody)) (failureRow (prim utf8_encode "E_INTERNAL" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "9" : Bytes) (ctor Failure.internal)) : Bytes) (define (prim utf8_encode "E_COUNT" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "10" : Bytes))),
  executable: true
}

  name: escapeNames,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat3 (define (prim utf8_encode "ESCAPE_PLAIN" : Bytes) (escapeText (ctor EscapeClass.plain))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ESCAPE_BACKSLASH" : Bytes) (escapeText (ctor EscapeClass.backslash))) (define (prim utf8_encode "ESCAPE_UNICODE" : Bytes) (escapeText (ctor EscapeClass.unicode)))),
  executable: true
}

  name: escapeCell,
  parameters: [octet : UInt8],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (escapeText (escapeClass octet)) (prim utf8_encode "," : Bytes) : Bytes),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext],
  executable: true

definition{
  name: escapeRow0,

definition{
  name: wordEscapeText,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (cat4 (cat3 (prim utf8_encode "// Carried text, with no theorem: the core test checks every byte in every lane.\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "static inline int word_needs_escape(uint64_t word) {\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "    const uint64_t low7 = 0x7f7f7f7f7f7f7f7fULL;\n" : Bytes)) (cat3 (prim utf8_encode "    uint64_t quote = word ^ 0x2222222222222222ULL, slash = word ^ 0x5c5c5c5c5c5c5c5cULL, control = word & 0xe0e0e0e0e0e0e0e0ULL;\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "#define HAS_ZERO(x) (~((((x) & low7) + low7) | (x) | low7))\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "    return (HAS_ZERO(quote) | HAS_ZERO(slash) | HAS_ZERO(control)) != 0;\n" : Bytes)) (prim utf8_encode "}\n" : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "" : Bytes)),
  executable: true
}

}

  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed 0x00 : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "README.md\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "# LexLean + C: a proved core on io_uring and SQLite B-tree reads\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "This entry is one [LexLean](https:/" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "github.com" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "UOR-Foundation" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "LexLean) program, compiled to the plain Lean in `lean/`. On the box, official Lean checks its theorems, runs it, and it writes the C that gets compiled. The decisions are written and proved in LexLean. The event loop, parsers and SQLite calls are C, carried as text through that program, because LexLean has no I" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "O or raw pointers. **The server as a whole is not formally verified.** The table below says what is.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| | |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "|---|---|\n" : Bytes) : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: section168,
  parameters: [seed : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed (prim utf8_encode "| Language | LexLean 0.3.0, compiled to Lean 4.32.1, for the core and for writing the files; C (gnu11) for everything that touches a socket or a pointer |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Framework | none |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Compiler | Official Lean 4.32.1 (SHA-256 pinned); GCC from `build-essential`, profile-guided, `-O3 -march=x86-64-v3` |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| io_uring | liburing 2.5 from Ubuntu 24.04, version pinned, linked statically |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| SQLite | 3.54.1 amalgamation, SHA-256 pinned, not patched, compiled into one translation unit with the server |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| **Nginx or direct** | **Direct** on `HOST:PORT` |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "## What is proved, tested, and neither\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Part | Status |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "|---|---|\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Batch order: a response leaves only after its batch commits, a failure response only after the commit fails | Proved over every phase and event |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Routes: only POST writes, every write needs a token, an unknown method is not found | Proved over every method and path shape |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Holding writes: reads go on while writes wait, a held connection keeps its order while the hold queue has room | Proved over every input |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| The write pause: closes at its limit, stays closed until the log is copied or the wait times out | Proved over every input |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| Errors: only an internal failure is a 5xx | Proved over every failure |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: section169,
  parameters: [seed : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed (prim utf8_encode "| JSON byte classes | Proved at bytes 0, 31, 32, 34, 92 and 255 only |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| The tables as they reach C, all 256 byte classes, the 8-byte escape test | Tested exhaustively, not proved |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "| The event loop, HTTP and JSON parsing, JWT, B-tree reads, SQLite calls | C. Not proved. Covered by `test" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "test.sh` and load runs |\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "The theorems are about the LexLean definitions. Nothing proves that the C uses a table correctly: a test checks that it reads them, and the suite exercises them. `build.sh` checks all 22 theorems with Lean's kernel every time it runs. The registered claims and planted defects are in [the repository](https:/" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "github.com" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "humuhumu33" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "uor-12USDserver).\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "If the hold queue is full, a later request on a held connection can overtake a held one. The theorem states this limit. It needs pipelined requests on a connection with a held write.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: section170,
  parameters: [seed : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed (prim utf8_encode "## How it is built\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "`install.sh` installs official Lean 4.32.1, about 2.8 GB on disk, used only by the build. `build.sh` then:\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "1. runs `lake build --wfail` in `lean/`, so a false theorem or a theorem left as `sorry` stops the build;\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "2. runs the program, which writes `generated" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "src" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "server.c`, `generated" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "src" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "core.h` and a copy of every other file here, and stops if any copy differs;\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "3. compiles the generated C with SQLite, downloading and checksumming SQLite.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: section171,
  parameters: [seed : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed (prim utf8_encode "`lean" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "UorEmit" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "Emit.lean` and `lean" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "LexLeanRuntime" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "Portable.lean` are generated by LexLean 0.3.0, which has no standalone release, from the `.lex.tex` source in the repository. `Portable.lean` is LexLean's runtime, MIT or Apache-2.0. Nothing prebuilt is shipped. Everything `Write.lean` reads is the Lean here.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "## Design\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "One thread runs the event loop, HTTP, JWT, JSON and SQLite reads. A second thread only runs WAL checkpoints.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **Sockets.** Direct descriptors, so `LimitNOFILE=65535` caps one ring's table and not the server. Up to 8 rings, made as connections need them. Eight `SO_REUSEPORT` listeners.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **Reading.** A multishot receive into provided buffers, 4,096 x 1 KB per ring, one submission fewer per request than a poll and a read. A receive that ends when buffers run out is re-armed in bounded rounds, to limit a storm of re-arms after a stall.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: section172,
  parameters: [seed : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed (prim utf8_encode "- **Drain first.** Requests are read out of their sockets as soon as they arrive, into a queue of up to 32,768, and answered up to 2,048 per batch. A request waiting in a socket costs 3-4 KB of network buffer; read out, it costs about 100 bytes.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **Batches.** Each batch is one SQLite transaction: reads from its snapshot, then writes through prepared statements, then `COMMIT`, then every response. No response leaves before its commit (rule 6).\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **Reads** walk SQLite's own B-tree cursors inside that transaction, with no SQL engine and no remembered data: the organiser allowed this for #153. The feed walks `posts_created_at_id_idx`, seeks the 20 posts, and counts likes with one range walk where it can. Every request reads SQLite while it is served (rule 5).\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **JWT.** Every token's signature is verified on every request. The HMAC key's pad states are hashed once at startup.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **WAL.** `synchronous=NORMAL`, `wal_autocheckpoint` off. A checkpoint thread at nice 19 runs passive checkpoints past 1,000 frames. Past 4,000, writes wait for at most 1 s while reads go on, so the next write can restart the log. Past 16,384 the loop checkpoints itself. A commit's frames go to the kernel in one `pwrite`, through a small VFS shim over `unix-excl`.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **Page cache.** 32 MB, with `mmap_size=0`.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "- **Code lock.** The binary is locked in RAM, so reclaim at the memory wall can't stall the loop on its own code.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "## Credit\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "Built on what earlier entries published, under MIT: the B-tree seek and record decoding follow lean-derive-hargup (#153); drain-first batching, the write gate, the one-write WAL idea and the reserved-field retry for Ubuntu's buffer-ring registration follow c-iouring-pavlyhalim (#245); the multishot receive follows rust-iouring-kensac (#264).\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "## Measurements\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "No user-count score is claimed. The organiser's benchmark is the only score. Everything measured so far ran on CI runners over loopback with an emulated CPU limit, which differs from the box in CPU, kernel and network.\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes),
  executable: true
}

  name: section173,
  parameters: [seed : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append (prim append seed (prim utf8_encode "## Run\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "```bash\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "sudo bash install.sh\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "bash build.sh\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "SQLITE_PATH=" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "path" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "to" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x2f : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "feed.db JWT_SECRET=twelve-dollar-challenge HOST=0.0.0.0 PORT=80 bash start.sh\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) (prim utf8_encode "```\n" : Bytes) : Bytes) 0x00 : Bytes),
  executable: true
}
